<header>An Open Entrance</header>
<meta>catchword: Mercu-</meta>

...than that with which Cadmus pierced the Serpent <note>original: "Cadmus Serpentem transfixit"; a mythological metaphor for stabilizing or "fixing" a volatile substance like mercury.</note>. Learn what the **Doves of Diana** are, which overcome the **Lion** by soothing him—the **Lion**, I say, the **Green** one <note>original: "Leonem Viridem"; often refers to a raw, acidic mineral solvent or an unrefined metallic ore</note>, who is truly the Babylonian Dragon, killing all things with its venom. Finally, learn the **Caduceus of Mercury** <note>The staff of Hermes, entwined with two serpents, representing the union of opposites</note>, with which he performs wonders, and who those nymphs are whom he fascinates with his enchantments, if you desire to obtain your wish.

## To Chapter II.

The Author rejects all the common purifications of Mercury; for salts, in the process of **sublimation** <term>Sublimation: Heating a solid until it turns to vapor and then collects as a solid again, used here to purify chemicals</term>, raise the pure along with the impure, and thus no separation of the **homogeneous** part from the **heterogeneous** <note>original: "partis homogeneæ, ab heterogenea"; separating substances of the same nature from those of a different, contaminating nature</note> occurs. The Philosophers, however, establish that Mercury is improved only in its own nature; therefore, it must be sublimed by itself until it is sufficiently freed from its **original stain**. Regarding how this Mercury is prepared, he speaks generally, saying that his Mercury is composed of various substances, yet of one **essence**. Just as, for example, Copper, Tin, and the other metals are distinct according to their **accidental form**, but are the same according to their **matter**, because all are made from Mercury. This Mercury is distinguished by the degrees of its own internal heat. The whole art, therefore, will be to extract that "hot Mercury" from the metals by means of common Mercury—that is, by that "fire" which is found in **Mars** <note>Iron</note> and the **Essence of Saturnia**—that is, of **Antimony**, which is called the **Saturn of the Philosophers**. Afterward, this **animated Mercury** receives the bond of Mercury—that is, **Luna** <note>Silver</note>—which connects these two extremes:

<column-break/>

<page-num>9</page-num>
<header>To the Closed Palace of the King.</header>
<sig>A 5</sig>
<meta>catchword: as</meta>

Mercury and Gold. But what is it that conquers all things, if not **Mars** <note>Iron</note>, who with the **Saturnian** substance, or **Antimony**, passes into **Chaos** <note>A term for the dark, confused initial mixture of the work</note>—that is, a mixture from which, in the form of a **Regulus** <term>Regulus: A metallic "button" or mass that settles at the bottom of a crucible during smelting</term>, Gold and Silver can be obtained without an **Elixir** or a **Tincture**; that is, <gloss>Gold or Silver will be obtained</gloss> from the Mercury of this Regulus. This Regulus is called "Chaos" because of the mutual mixing of things; it is called "**our Moon**" because silver can be extracted from it; it is called the **Magnet** and the **Steel**, because Antimony, like a Magnet, attracts to itself the best substance of the iron. But these individual names apply to our Regulus, though not all at once; for there are many preparations that come before the "King’s Bath" is made from the Mercury, and it receives different names according to the variety of those preparations.

One must know, therefore, who are the **Companions of Cadmus**, or the **Companions of the Fire**, who are required for heating the Mercury; these are the Metals. The **Serpent** that devours them is Mercury. The **Hollow Oaks** are the ashes surrounding the glass vessel, where in the process of **digestion** <note>A slow, steady heating process</note> Cadmus—that is, the **Sulphur** or the **Essence of Gold**—penetrates and fixes the Mercury. The **Doves of Diana** are two parts of **Marcasite** <note>A mineral often confused with pyrite or used here to refer to a specific metallic starter</note>, which soothe the **Green Lion**—that is, the Regulus, which is immature and therefore called "green." One must also know how Mercury, through the **Caduceus**—that is, through its peaceful nature—composes all these things with one another so that one thing is made from them all. The **Nymphs** are **Venus** <note>Copper</note> and **Luna** <note>Silver</note>. In this chapter, he names in a general way the materials required for the whole work of the **Philosophical Mercury**: namely, Mercury, Mars, Antimony, Silver, Venus, and the other metals.

...per as well as **Marcasite** <term>Marcasite: Historically, this term referred to various shiny minerals like pyrite or bismuth ore, often thought to contain the "seed" of metals</term>, with a **Fire of Ashes** <note>original: "Igne Cinerum"; a gentle, indirect heat achieved by placing the vessel in a bed of warm ashes</note>, each of which must soon be treated individually.

## CHAPTER III.
### *Concerning the Steel of the Sages.*

The Wise **Magi** <note>The "Magi" here refers to the legendary masters of alchemy, not just the biblical figures</note> handed down many things to posterity concerning their **Steel**, and they attributed no small importance to it; for this reason, there is no small conflict among the common crowd of Alchemists as to what exactly should be understood by the name "Steel." Various men have given various interpretations of this. The Author of the **New Light** <note>original: "Novi Luminis"; a reference to Michael Sendivogius, a famous 17th-century alchemist</note> wrote about this candidly, yet obscurely. I, so as not to hide anything from the seekers of the Art out of envy, shall describe it sincerely. 

Our **Steel** <note>original: "Chalybs"; in alchemy, this is rarely literal steel but rather a specific mineral agent used to "sharpen" or activate mercury</note> is the true key of our Work, without which the fire of the lamp can be lit by no art: it is the **mineral of Gold**, a spirit exceedingly pure above all others, it is a secret, infernal fire, highly volatile in its kind, a miracle of the world, a system of the superior virtues within the inferior things. For this reason, the Almighty marked it with a notable sign, whose birth is announced through the East on the horizon of its hemisphere by **Phosphorus** <note>original: "Phosphorum"; the "Light-Bringer" or Morning Star, here used as a metaphor for the appearance of a specific crystalline structure in the crucible</note>. The Magi saw it in the Dawn and were stunned, and immediately recognized the most Serene King born in the world. You, when you have glimpsed his Stars, follow them even to the Cradle; there you will see—

---

<page-num>11</page-num>
<header>To the Closed Palace of the King.</header>
<meta>catchword: who</meta>

—you will see the beautiful infant. By removing the filth, honor the Royal Child; open your treasury, offer the gift of Gold; thus finally after death he will give you his flesh and blood, the highest **Medicine** in the three kingdoms of the earth.

->*Regarding Chapter III.*<-

The core of this Chapter is to teach how the **Regulus** <term>Regulus: Meaning "Little King," this is the metallic heart of an ore that settles at the bottom of a crucible, often forming a star-like pattern on its surface</term> is to be prepared, which is the Key of our Work. For through this, **Mercury** is animated; for this is the **Sulfur of Mars** <note>original: "Sulphur Martis"; Mars is the alchemical name for Iron; this "Sulfur" is the active, combustible principle extracted from it</note>, the hot, volatile Essence of Gold, which God has signed with a Star in the Philosophical East of the King. That is to say, in the preparation or Birth of the Regulus. Seeing this Star, the Magi said the Royal Child was born; the Philosophers, seeing this, said the Regulus was born. When united with Mercury, it animates the Mercury; to this animated Mercury, Gold is added and committed to **digestion** <term>Digestion: A process of slow, constant heating used to mature or "ripen" a substance over time</term>.

## CHAPTER IV.
### *Concerning the Magnet of the Sages.*

Just as steel is drawn to the magnet, and the magnet spontaneously turns itself toward the steel, so also the **Magnet of the Sages** draws their Steel. Therefore, just as I have taught that the Steel is the Mineral of Gold, likewise our Magnet is the true Mineral of our Steel. I further notify you that our Magnet has a hidden center, abundant in **Salt**, which—

It uses the metaphor of the Star of Bethlehem to describe the distinctive star-like crystalline pattern that appears when this substance is successfully prepared.</summary>

...which **Salt** is the **solvent** <term>menstruum: a philosophical solvent capable of dissolving metals without destroying their "seed"</term> in the **Sphere of the Moon** <note>original: "Sphæra Lunæ"; likely referring to the purified state of silver or the white stage of the work</note>, which knows how to **calcine** <term>calcine: to reduce a substance to a powder by heat</term> gold. This center turns itself with an archetypal desire toward the **Pole**, in which the power of the **Steel** is exalted by degrees. In the Pole is the heart of **Mercury**, which is the true fire, in which there is the rest of its Lord; sailing through this great sea so that it might reach both **Indias** <note>A metaphor for the two goals of alchemy: the White Stone (East India) and the Red Stone (West India)</note>, it directs its course by the sight of the **North Star**, which our **Magnet** will make appear to you. The wise man will rejoice, yet the fool will value these things little, nor will he learn wisdom, even though he might see the central Pole turned outward and marked with the **notable sign of the Almighty** <note>This refers to the "Star of Antimony," a star-shaped crystalline pattern that forms on the surface of purified antimony as it cools</note>. They are of such stiff necks that even if they see Signs and Miracles, they will not lay aside their fallacies, nor enter the right path.

### On Chapter IV.

In this Chapter, the Author understands **Antimony** as the **Magnet**, which draws the **Steel** to itself, through whose Salt **Mercury** is animated; here the Magnet is called Antimony or **Aries** <note>The Ram, the first sign of the zodiac</note>, in whose belly lives the **North Star**—that is, **Mars**, who lives in the House of Aries—which two are united and marked with the **Starry Seal** <note>original: "Sigillo Stellare"; the "Regulus of Antimony," a metallic form of antimony that crystallizes in a star pattern</note>.

## CHAPTER V.
### The Chaos of the Sages.

Let the student of the Philosophers hear the Sages unanimously concluding that this Work is to be likened to the **Creation of the universe**. In the beginning, therefore, **GOD** created the **Heaven** and the **Earth**, and the **Earth** was empty and void, and the **Spirit of God** moved upon the face of the waters, and God said, "Let there be light," and there was light. These words will be enough for a **Son of the Art**. For it is necessary for **Heaven** to be joined with **Earth** upon the bed of friendship. Thus shall he reign in honor throughout his whole life. 

**Earth** is a heavy body, the mother of minerals, which boils secretly within itself, although it brings forth trees and animals into the light. **Heaven** is that in which the great lights revolve with the stars, and it sends its powers through the air to the things below; but in the beginning, all things confused together made a **Chaos**. Behold, I have holy revealed the truth: for our **Chaos** is like a mineral earth in respect to its coagulation, and yet it is a volatile air, within which is the **heaven of the Philosophers** in its center—which center is truly astral, radiating through the earth even to the surface with its brilliance. And who is a **Magus** so wise, that from these things he may gather that a **new King** is born?

It then frames the alchemical process as a microcosm of the Biblical Creation, where a "Chaos" must be organized into a new, perfected state.</summary>

->On Chapter V.<-

Here the most experienced Author teaches that the **Regulus** <term>regulus: the metallic heart of a mineral; here, specifically the purified part of antimony</term> is the Earth, while Heaven is **Mercury**, in which the great lights <note>The Sun (gold) and Moon (silver)</note> revolve; this happens in Mercury, to which we join the **Sun**, **Moon**, **Mars** <note>Iron</note>, and other Metals, which are the Lights of Heaven. The heavenly Mercurial Air penetrates all of these and extracts the best Nature from them, so that it may thereafter be called **animated** <note>original: "animatus"; meaning the mercury has been "given life" or charged with the metallic virtues</note>.

### CHAPTER VI.
### *The Air of the Wise.*

The "expanse" or "firmament" is called **Air** in the Holy Scriptures. This Air is likewise named our **Chaos**, and this is not without a significant secret; for just as the atmospheric air is the separator of the waters, so too is our "air." Therefore, our work is in truth a system of the **macrocosm** <note>original: "majoris mundi"; the "greater world" or universe</note>,

<column-break/>

<page-num>15</page-num>
<header>To the Closed Palace of the King.</header>
<meta>catchword: Arseni-</meta>

world. Since, just as the Waters beneath the firmament are seen and appear to us who live above the earth, yet the higher Waters escape our sight because they are so far distant from us; in the same way, in our **microcosm** <note>The "small world" created within the alchemist's vessel</note>, there are extra-central mineral waters which are visible; but those which are shut within escape our sight, and yet they truly exist. These are those of which the Author of *The New Light* <note>Referring to Michael Sendivogius, a famous 17th-century alchemist</note> says: "they exist, but do not appear until it pleases the Artist." Just as, therefore, the Air distinguishes between the waters, so too our "air" prevents all entry of the extra-central waters into the Waters that are in the center. For if they were to enter and be mixed, they would immediately grow together in an indissoluble union. I say, therefore, that the external, vaporous, burning **Sulphur** <note>In this context, an impurity that "burns" or destroys the metal</note> clings stubbornly to our Chaos; being unable to resist its tyranny, the pure part flies away from the fire in the form of a dry powder. If you know how to water this dry earth with the water of its own kind, you will loosen the pores of the earth, and this external **thief** <note>A common alchemical term for an impurity or a substance that "steals" the power of the work</note> with the workers of iniquity will be cast out; the water will be purged of its leprous filth and superfluous dropsical moisture by the addition of the **True Sulphur**, and you will have in your possession the **Trevian Assembly** <note>original: "Comitia Trevisfontina"; likely a poetic reference to the "Fountain of Trevisan," a symbol of the purifying mercurial bath named after the alchemist Bernard of Trevisan</note>, whose waters are specifically dedicated to the **Virgin Diana** <note>The Moon/Silver; representing the white stage of the process</note>. This thief is the wicked **Arseni-** <note>The text cuts off here; the catchword indicates "Arsenicum" or Arsenic</note>

...armed with arsenical malignancy, which the Winged Youth <note>A poetic reference to volatile Mercury</note> dreads and flees. And although the "central water" is his bride, yet he does not dare to exert his most burning love toward her on account of the snares of the thief, whose tricks are almost unavoidable. May Diana <note>In alchemy, Diana often represents the moon or purified silver, known for her cooling and purifying properties</note> be propitious to you here, for she knows how to tame wild beasts; her **Two Doves** will temper the malignancy of the air with their wings. Through these pores the youth easily enters, immediately shaking it through the <unclear>oledos</unclear> <note>Possibly a corruption of "olida" (smelly things) or a specific chemical term for vessels</note>, and raises a gloomy cloud. Thus, at God's command, the light will appear. Separate the light from the darkness for the seventh time, and this "sophic" creation of Mercury will be complete; the seventh day shall be for you a Sabbath of rest, from which time until the revolution of a year you may expect the generation of the son of the supernatural Sun, who shall come into the world at the end of the ages to deliver his brothers from every stain.

### To Chapter VI.

The mixing of Mercury with the **Regulus of Martial Antimony** <term>Regulus of Mars-Antimony: A metallic substance produced by reducing antimony ore with iron (Mars)</term> is called **Chaos**, on account of the confusion of different things, which the Mercurial Air penetrates and permeates. Furthermore, it must be known that "Double Waters" are given 

<column-break/>

->To the Closed Palace of the King.<-
<sig>B</sig>
<meta>catchword: of the action</meta>

in this Operation. One Water is the Mercury itself which appears and is seen; the other Water—or the other Mercury—is in the Regulus, which does not appear until this Regulus is in some way resuscitated by the air latent in the Mercury. These are the Waters which do not appear until it pleases the Artist. Furthermore, it must be known that our Regulus has **Arsenical Dross** <gloss>original: Feces Arsenicales</gloss> which cannot be separated from it except by a Water of its own kind—that is, the Mercury which seizes that which is of its own Nature and rejects the Arsenical Dross. 

Moreover, although this Mercury (or metallic water) is betrothed to the Regulus, they can never be composed with one another—that is, they have no entry into one another—on account of the Arsenical Dross. Therefore, the **Two Doves of Diana** will temper 그 evil, and by means of them—that is, through the double **Marcasite** <term>Marcasite: A term used here for a specific metallic mineral or flux used to facilitate the union of metals</term>—the Regulus will easily be united to the Mercury. Once joined in **Amalgamation**, it rejects all **Arsenical Sulfur**, which must be washed away completely with Waters until the Amalgam becomes white. This Light (or this Mercury amalgamated with the Regulus) must be separated from each other by a **Retort** seven times, and thus the **Animated Mercury of the Philosophers** will be obtained.

## CHAPTER VII.
### Concerning the first Operation of preparing Sophic Mercury through the Flying Eagles.

Know, Brother, that the exact preparation of the **Philosophical Eagles** <note>Eagles: A code word for the stages of sublimation or distillation</note> is the first [step] of perfection...

...grade of the action is considered, in the knowing of which a skillful genius is required. For do not believe that this Science has come to any of us by chance, or by a lucky imagination—as the stupidly ignorant common people believe—but rather we have sweated long and hard, spent many sleepless nights, and suffered much labor and the sweat of smoke, that we might attain the truth. Therefore, Studious Apprentice <note>original: Tyro Studiose</note>, know for certain that you will achieve nothing without sweat and labor, particularly in the First Work, even though in the Second Work nature alone performs the task without any laying on of hands, with only moderate external Heat <note>original: Igne; literally "Fire"</note> applied. 

Understand therefore, Brother, the sayings of the Sages <note>original: Sophorum</note>, when they write that their **Eagles** <note>In alchemy, "Eagles" represent the process of sublimation or the volatility of Mercury</note> are to be led to devour the **Lion** <note>The "Lion" usually refers to the fixed metallic substance, such as the Regulus of Antimony or Gold, which must be dissolved</note>; the fewer their number, the harder the struggle and the slower the victory, but the work is most excellently completed with the number Seven or Nine. Consider that the **Sophic Mercury** is the **Bird of Hermes**, which is called now a Goose, now a Pheasant, now this, now that. However, when the Magi speak of their Eagles, they speak in the plural and assign a number from three to ten. Yet they do not wish to be understood as if they wanted that many weights of Water to one of Earth, but rather their sayings ought to be interpreted regarding the internal weight—

<column-break/>

<page-num>19</page-num>
# ->To the King's Closed Palace<-
<sig>B 2</sig>
<meta>catchword: patia-</meta>

—it is necessary that the Water be taken "sharpened" as many times as they count the Eagles; which Sharpening <note>original: Acuatio</note> is done through Sublimation. Let each individual Sublimation of the Philosophers' Mercury be one Eagle, and the Seventh Sublimation will so exalt your Mercury that the **King's Bath** <note>original: Balneum Regis; a stage where the metal is dissolved in the prepared mercury</note> shall become most suitable. 

Therefore, so that you may have this knot properly untied, prick up your ears most attentively: Let there be taken four parts of our **Fiery Dragon** <note>A reference to the acidic or "burning" mineral spirit</note>, who hides the **Magical Steel** <term>Chalybs Magicus: A secret alchemical agent, often associated with the "star" of the Regulus of Mars-Antimony, used to attract the soul of the metal</term> in his belly, and nine parts of our **Magnet** <note>The "Magnet" refers to the substance that attracts the desired metallic seed</note>; mix them together by means of the parched **Vulcan** <note>Fire or the heat of the furnace</note>, in the form of Mineral Water, upon which a scum will float that must be cast away. Reject the shell and choose the kernel; purify it a third time by Fire and Salt, which will be easily done if **Saturn** <note>Lead or the base mineral form</note> beholds his own form in the **Mirror of Mars** <note>Iron; this refers to a specific crystalline pattern called the "star" that appears on the surface of purified antimony when iron is used in its reduction</note>. 

From this will come the **Chameleon** or our **Chaos**, in which all secrets lie hidden by virtue, not yet in act. This is the **Hermaphroditic Infant**, who from his very first swaddling clothes has been infected by the bite of a rabid **Corascene Dog** <note>original: Canem Corascenum; a poetic name for a poisonous or darkening agent in the initial stage</note>, whence he grows foolish and insane with a perpetual **Hydrophobia** <note>Fear of water; here implying the substance's resistance to being dissolved or combined with the "water" of the mercury</note>; indeed, though Water is closer to him than any other natural thing, yet he dreads and flees from it. O fates! Yet there are in the **Grove of Diana** **Two Doves** which soothe his insane madness; then, lest he suffer a relapse of hydrophobia...

...suffer, you should submerge it in the **Waters**, so that it might perish in them; the **Black Mad Dog** <note>A symbol for the crude impurities in the mineral matter, likely related to antimony</note>, unable to endure these waters, will rise to the surface nearly suffocated. ### To Chapter VII.

In this Chapter, he teaches the twofold **Animation of Mercury**, where he marks the **Fiery Dragon** as **Mars** <note>Iron</note>, who has in his Belly—that is, in his Center—a certain fiery **Essence** which the **Magnet** draws to itself; that is, **Antimony**, which two purified natures are called the **Hermaphrodite** <note>A "rebis" or dual-natured substance combining "male" (sulfur/Mars) and "female" (mercury/antimony) qualities</note>, meaning one thing forged from two natures. This is the **Regulus** <note>The metallic heart of a mineral</note> which, because of its **Arsenical Sulfur**, is said to be "bitten by the Mad Dog." For this reason, it cannot be joined to **Mercury** (or its Water) except through the mediation of **Marcasite** <note>In this context, likely referring to bismuth or a specific metallic ore used as a flux</note>. When these two are amalgamated together, they turn black. This **Blackness** <note>The "Nigredo" or first stage of the work, symbolizing decay and rebirth</note> must be washed away, so that afterwards a **Separation** may occur via the **Retort**, to be repeated seven or ten times. **Mercury** (that is, the **Eagle**) will fly, leaving behind the dead **Doves of Diana**, which have no life-restoring power because Mercury, which is the Life,

## CHAPTER VIII.
### Concerning the labor and tedium of the first preparation.

Ignorant "little chemists" <note>original: Chemicolæ; a derogatory term for those who lack true philosophical understanding</note> dream indeed that the whole work, from start to finish, is mere recreation full of delight, and they place labor outside the boundaries of this Art. But let them safely enjoy their own opinion! In the Work which they have imagined to be so easy, they will surely reap a hollow harvest from their idle operation. We know, for instance, that after the **Divine Blessing** and a good **Root**, **Labor**, **Industry**, and **Diligence** hold the primary place. Nor will an easy labor (which should rather be considered play or mental recreation) grant what we so greatly desire according to our wishes. Indeed, as **Hermes** says, one must spare neither soul nor labor. Otherwise, what the **Wise Man** foretold in parables will be verified: namely, that "the desire of the slothful shall kill him." It is no wonder if so many people handling **Alchemy** are reduced to poverty; they flee from **Labor**, you see,

To make the mass fit for use, says the Poet, *this is the Work, this is the Labor.* <note>original: "hoc Opus, hic Labor est"; a famous line from Virgil’s Aeneid regarding the difficulty of returning from the underworld</note> And again:

> *One seeks the gilded fleece from the well-known summit, etc.*

For in our starting materials there are many heterogeneous superfluities <note>impurities or substances that are not of the same "nature" as the goal</note>, which can never (for the purpose of our Work) be reduced to purity; therefore, it is expedient to purge them thoroughly. This will be impossible to do without the **Theory of our Secrets**, by which we teach the medium through which the **royal diadem** <note>a symbol for the purified gold or the Philosopher’s Stone</note> is extracted from the **prostitute’s menstruum** <note>original: "meretricis menstruo"; a startling alchemical term for the crude, impure mineral or solvent used as a starting material, often associated with antimony</note>. ## CHAPTER IX.
### On the Power of Our Mercury over All Metals.

Our **Mercury** is that **Serpent** which devoured the companions of **Cadmus** <note>In mythology, Cadmus slew a dragon/serpent; in alchemy, this represents the interaction between the metallic "fixed" principle and the "volatile" mercury</note>. This is no wonder, for it had first devoured Cadmus himself, who was stronger than the others. Yet, in the end, Cadmus will transfix this Serpent when he has coagulated it with the virtue of his **Sulfur**. Know, therefore, that this Mercury of ours dominates all **Metallic Bodies**, and dissolves them into their proximate **Mercurial Matter**, by separating their **Sulfurs**. Know also, that the Mercury of one or two **Eagles** <note>An "Eagle" refers to one cycle of sublimation or distillation; the more "Eagles," the more purified and potent the mercury becomes</note>...

### On Chapter IX.

In the seventh chapter, the preparation of Philosophical Mercury was explained. If Gold is added to this Mercury, the effect of the Gold-making Powder <note>The Philosopher's Stone</note> will be obtained via the Longer Way in a year and a half. However, if anyone intends an abbreviation <note>the "Short Way," which is faster but more dangerous and difficult</note> of the Work, it is necessary that that Mercury—which devoured Cadmus’s companions <note>A mythological allegory for the purification of the raw materials</note>, that is, Saturn <note>Lead</note> and Mars <note>Iron</note> in the Regulus <note>The metallic heart or "button" found at the bottom of the crucible after smelting</note>—should also receive other metals into itself. For instance, if it is Mercury animated once, twice, or thrice, it is powerful enough to reduce Saturn, Jupiter, and Venus into Mercury, so that it might be rendered hotter. From the third sharpening <note>acuitione: the process of increasing the chemical "hunger" or activity of the mercury</note> up to the seventh, it is powerful enough to resolve the Moon <note>Silver</note>; from the seventh to the tenth, the Sun <note>Gold</note>. Indeed, to put it in a single word, with this Mercury one can obtain a perpetual Mine of Saturn, Jupiter, Venus, the Moon, and the Sun.

### CHAPTER X.
## Concerning the Sulfur which is in the Sophic Mercury.

Doubtless, above all else, there is this: that in our Mercury, there is not only actual but also active Sulfur; and yet it retains all the proportions and the form of Mercury. Therefore, it is necessary that a form be introduced to it through our preparation, which form is the Metallic Sulfur; this Sulfur is the Fire that putrefies the composite Sun <note>Gold in its alchemical state</note>. This Sulfurous Fire is the Spiritual Seed which our Virgin <note>Philosophical Mercury, often called the "Virgin" because of its receptive and pure nature</note>—nonetheless remaining undefiled—has contracted, because she can admit Spiritual Love. This is virginity uncorrupted, according to the author of the **Hermetic Secret** <note>Likely Jean d'Espagnet, author of "Arcanum Hermeticum"</note> and Experience itself. By reason of this Sulfur, it is a Hermaphrodite, because it includes both the active and the passive Principle at the same time; the same Mercury, through the same degree of digestion, becomes visible. Indeed, when joined with the Sun <note>Gold</note>, it softens, liquefies, and dissolves it, with the heat tempered to the requirements of the composition. By the same Fire, it coagulates itself and produces Gold <note>original: "Solem"; the Sun is the alchemical symbol for Gold</note> in its coagulation, and does so according to the pleasure of the operation. This will perhaps seem incredible to you;

It does this in the manner of the "Flower of Milk" <note>likely referring to the way cream rises and thickens</note>, with a subtle Earth, as it were, floating upon the Waters. However, if it is joined to Gold that is already perfect, it does not then coagulate, but instead dissolves the Corporate Gold; once dissolved, it remains with the Gold under a single form. Nevertheless, before this perfect union, death must necessarily come first, so that after Death they may be united—not simply in a single perfection, but in a "thousand-fold" perfection that is more than perfect.

# CHAPTER XI.
## *Concerning the Discovery of the Perfect Mastery.*

The Sages of old, as many as achieved this Art without the help of books, were led to attain it in this way by the will of God. For I cannot persuade myself that it came to them through immediate Revelation—unless perhaps Solomon possessed it, which I would rather leave to the judgment of others than determine myself. And yet, even if he did possess it, what prevents him from having reached it through investigation? For he asked only for Wisdom, which God gave him in such a way that with it he also possessed Riches and Peace. Therefore, if he searched into the nature of plants and trees, from the Cedar in Lebanon even unto the Hyssop that grows on the wall <note>A reference to 1 Kings 4:33, illustrating Solomon's vast natural knowledge</note>, who of sound mind will deny that he likewise understood the natures of Minerals, the knowledge of which is no less pleasant? But to the point. We say that it is likely to be believed that the first Adepts who possessed this Mastery—among whom was Hermes <note>Hermes Trismegistus, the legendary founding figure of Alchemy</note>—lacked an abundance of books,

[pious men] <note>original: "pia"; completing the thought that these were practitioners seeking a sacred or natural truth</note> sought at first nothing more than perfection, but rather the simple exaltation of imperfect things to the Royal state <note>The "Royal state" refers to the transformation of base metals into Gold</note>. Since they saw that all metallic things are of Mercurial origin, and that Mercury is most similar to Gold—the most perfect of metals—in Weight and Homogeneity, they attempted to "digest" this Mercury until it reached the maturity of Gold; however, they could not achieve this with any Fire. Therefore, they reasoned that besides external Heat, an internal Fire was required to fulfill their wishes. They sought this internal fire in many things: first, they distilled highly hot Waters from lesser minerals, and with these they corroded the Mercury. Yet by no Art or Way could they succeed in making the Mercury change its internal properties, because all these Corrosive Waters were only external Agents, acting in the manner of a fire, though differently; but these Menstrua <term>Menstrua: Alchemical solvents, named by analogy to the menstrual cycle as they were seen as the medium that dissolves the "seed" to produce new life</term>, as they called them, did not remain joined with the dissolved Body. Confirmed in this reasoning, they rejected all Salts, except for one Salt, which is the First Being of Salts <note>original: "Salium Ens primum"; likely referring to a specific mineral salt or "philosophical salt" believed to be the root of all others</note>, which dissolves any metal and by that same action coagulates Mercury; but it does so only by a violent Way. Thus, an Agent of that kind is separated from the substances again with its weight and power intact. Therefore they realized

at last, these Wise Men, that the watery Crudities and earthy Dregs <note>original: "Fæces terreas"</note> in Mercury stand in the way of its digestion. They knew, I say, that if Mercury could strip off these dregs, it would immediately become fixed <term>Fixed: In alchemy, a substance that is "fixed" can endure the heat of a fire without evaporating or changing</term>. For it has within itself a fermenting Sulfur <term>Sulfur: The active, masculine, and "burning" principle in alchemy that provides the "seed" of form to the "womb" of Mercury</term>, of which even the smallest grain would be enough to coagulate the entire Mercurial Body, provided the Dregs and Crudities could be removed. They attempted this through various Purifications, but in vain; for the aforementioned Work requires both Mortification <term>Mortification: The symbolic "death" or chemical breakdown of a substance into a dark, powerless state</term> and Regeneration, for which an Agent is necessary. Finally, they understood that Mercury in the bowels of the Earth was destined to become Metal; it maintained a daily Motion toward this goal as long as the fitness of the Place and other external conditions remained well-disposed. But when these were accidentally corrupted, this immature Offspring fell away of its own accord. Thus, what is seen is something deprived of Motion and Life; and from Deprivation back to a State of Being, an immediate return is impossible. The Passive, you see, is in the Mercury what ought to have been the Active; so that it is necessary to introduce another Life of the same Nature into it, and in this introduction, the latent Life of the Mercury is awakened. But they proved by experiment that this also retained its own filth, and they remembered the well-worn proverb: **"Be clean yourself, you who desire to cleanse another."** <note>original: "Mundus esto, qui alterum cupis mundificare"</note> Therefore, attempting to purge this, they found it entirely impossible, since it had no metallic Sulfur in itself, even though it abounded in the most purified **Salt of Nature**. Thus they noted that while there was a small and only passive Sulfur in Mercury, they found none that was actual in this Offspring of Saturn, but only potential Sulfur. For this reason, it entered into a pact with burning Arsenical Sulfur, and without this "folly" it cannot exist in a coagulated form; and yet it is so dull that it prefers to live with this Enemy, by whom it is most closely imprisoned, and to commit "fornication" rather than renounce it and appear in a Mercurial form. Then the Sages immediately rejoiced, when they saw that Sulfur was not only discovered, but prepared. After the rains, it has become clear once more. From this emerged the **Hermaphroditic Mercury**. <note>original: "Mercurius Hermaphroditicus"; this represents the union of the "male" (sulfur/active) and "female" (mercury/passive) principles into a single substance</note> Therefore, they placed this in the Fire, and in a time not at all long, they coagulated it, and in its **Coagulation** they found the purest **Gold** and **Silver**. <note>original: "Solem Lunamque"; literally "Sun and Moon," the standard alchemical symbols for Gold and Silver</note> At last, returning to their senses, they realized that the Mercury assigned in this way, not yet coagulated, was not yet a Metal, but was volatile enough that it would leave no residue behind in its distillation. For this reason, they named it their **immature Gold** and their **living Silver**. They also considered that since it was the true **First Essence of Gold**, <term>First Essence: "Ens primum," the fundamental, spiritual reality of a substance before it takes physical form</term> while it still existed in a volatile state, why should it not be the "field" in which the **Sown Gold** might be increased in power? For that reason, they placed Gold within it, and—what easily produces wonderment—that which was fixed was made **Volatile** in the same substance; the hard became soft, and the coagulated was dissolved, while nature itself stood amazed. Therefore, they wedded these two to one another, enclosed them in Glass, placed them at the Fire, and governed the Work according to the requirements of Nature over a long time. Thus the dead was brought to life, and the living was made dead; the body decayed, and the **Spirit** rose again in glory, and at last the **Soul** was exalted into the **Quintessence**, <term>Quintessence: The "fifth essence" or highest purity of a substance, believed to be a universal medicine</term> the supreme medicine for Animals, Metals, and Vegetables.

### Regarding Chapter XI.

The ancient Philosophers sought especially how they might make the **Perfect** from the **Imperfect**—that is, how they might make Gold or Silver from Mercury. Afterward, they investigated how they might make the **More-than-Perfect** <note>original: "Plusquamperfectum"; referring to the Philosopher's Stone, which is more perfect than gold because it can transform other things into gold</note> from the Perfect. First, they sought how Mercury must be fixed through corrosive waters, but until now they labored in vain, because the imperfection of Mercury proceeds from a lack of good **Sulfur**, or from an internal cause, whereas waters and salts act only externally, or upon the surface; therefore, this evil could not be cured. They did indeed find the **First Essence of Salts**, which is found everywhere and gives life to all things—namely, **Saltpeter** <note>original: "Sal Nitrum"</note>—which can coagulate Mercury, but only externally. After the coagulation of the Mercury, it is called "coagulated Mercury" but it is not "fixed," because **Fixation**, strictly speaking, proceeds from an internal Agent. Afterward, they purged the Mercury, but even this does not suffice, because Heat (or the internal Agent of the Mercury) must be introduced. This **Life**, or active Sulfur, consists in a metallic, spiritual **Seed**. It is sought in vain in **Copper** <note>original: "Venere" (Venus)</note> just as it is in **Lead** <note>original: "Saturno" (Saturn)</note>, for their Mercury is coagulated by an arsenical sulfur. This Sulfur is found in the **House of Aries**; <term>House of Aries: In alchemy, this refers to Iron (Mars), as Aries is the astrological house of the planet Mars</term> in this House, **Mars** dwells, whom the **Magnet** draws to himself. This Metallic Matter is marked with the **Royal Starry Seal**, <note>A reference to the "Star Regulus," a star-shaped crystalline pattern that forms on the surface of antimony when purified with iron</note> yet it has no entry into the Mercury except through **Marcasite**. <note>original: "Marcasitam"; likely referring to Bismuth or a specific metallic ore used as a mediator</note> From this, the Mercury comes forth "animated"; passing into a mineral state, it will give Gold and Silver in its Coagulation—that is, it will give Simple Perfection. If, however, the Gold should be...

### CHAPTER XII.
### *Concerning the Manner of creating the perfect Mastery in general.*

We ought to give immortal thanks to God, that He has shown us these Secrets of Nature <note>original: "Arcana Naturae"</note> which He has hidden from the eyes of many. Therefore, those things which have been freely given to us by that great Giver, we shall freely and faithfully reveal to other Students. Know then that the greatest Secret of our Work is nothing other than the **Cohobation** <term>Cohobation: A process where a liquid is repeatedly distilled from its solid residue, pouring it back over the remains to further extract its essence.</term> of natures, one upon another, until the most digested Virtue is extracted from the digested substance by the crude one. For this, however, there is required: First, the exact Comparison, Preparation, and Adaptation of the things entering the Work. Second, a good Disposition of external factors; Third, a good **Regimen** <note>original: "Regimen"; the careful management of heat and timing in the furnace</note> is required for the things thus prepared; Fourth, a Foreknowledge of the **Colors** appearing in the Work is desired, lest one proceed blindly; Fifth, **Patience**, lest the Work be hurried or managed precipitously. Of all these things, we shall speak in order, as a brother to a brother.

### CHAPTER XIII.
### *On the Use of Mature Sulfur in the Work of the Elixir.*

We have spoken of the necessity of Mercury, and have handed down many Secrets of Mercury, which before me were quite meager <note>original: "jejuna"</note> in the world, because almost all chemical books teem with either obscure Riddles, or Sophistical Operations, or finally a heap of rough words. But I have not acted thus, resigning my will in this matter to the divine pleasure, who, in this final period of the world, seems to me about to unlock these Treasures; for which reason I no longer fear lest the Art become cheap—far from it. This cannot happen. For true Wisdom maintains itself in eternal honor. I wish that Gold and Silver—that great **Idol** worshipped by the whole world until now—would finally become as cheap as dung! Then we, who understand these things, would not strive so much to remain hidden, we who now [flee] the very [punishment] of Cain... <note>The text likely refers to the "mark of Cain," suggesting the alchemist lives as a wandering exile or fears being hunted for his secrets.</note>

<summary>This page introduces the practical requirements for the Alchemical "Mastery," emphasizing "Cohobation" (repeated distillation) and the author's intent to reveal these secrets clearly for the first time. Philalethes expresses a philosophical hope that the discovery of the Elixir would make gold and silver so common they lose their status as idols.</summary>

# An Open Entrance
<meta>catchword: age</meta>

...to one who says he would do this or that if he possessed the **Stone**, I would say: "You are an acquaintance of a certain **Adept** <term>Adept: A person who has mastered the 'Great Work' of alchemy and produced the Stone.</term>," and immediately reflecting in his mind, he would answer: "This is impossible; perhaps I might see one once, but to move in his inner circle is not possible, for I would scent him out." 

You who believe this of yourself—do you not believe others possess equal perspicacity to discern you? For you must associate with some people, otherwise you will seem like another **Diogenes the Cynic** <note>Diogenes (c. 412–323 BCE) was a Greek philosopher known for living in a ceramic jar and shunning social norms to prove his self-sufficiency.</note>. Yet if you associate with commoners, it is unworthy of your station. If you contract friendships among the wise, you must be extremely cautious lest they discern you with the same ease that you believe you could "fish out" another **Adept** (to whom the Secret is known, though unknown to you) if only you could enjoy his familiar company. 

You will not easily recognize a suspicion once it is conceived until it causes you grave trouble; even a slight conjecture will suffice for others to set traps for you. Such is the wickedness in Men that we have known some **strangled with a rope** who were nevertheless strangers to the **Art** <note>original: "ab Arte"; refers to the Art of Alchemy.</note>. It was enough that certain desperate men had heard a rumor about such an **Art**, for which those victims had a reputation for skill. It would be tedious to recount all that we ourselves have experienced, seen, and heard on this matter, especially in this

**age** of the world, more than any before. Who does not pretend to **Alchemy**? This caution of yours will incite a zeal in some to pry more deeply into you; they will snarl about the **Sophistication of Coins** <note>original: "Nummorum Sophisticatione"; the debasing or counterfeiting of currency, a crime often associated with alchemists.</note>, and what not? 

But if you are a little more open, your effects are unusual, whether they be in Medicine or in **Alchemy**. If you possess a huge weight of **Gold** and **Silver** and wish to sell it, anyone will easily wonder where such a great quantity of the **purest Gold** <note>original: "Auri Obryzi"; refined, high-purity gold.</note> or finest **Silver** could be brought from, since it is brought from almost no places—except perhaps **Barbary** or **Guinea** <note>In the 17th century, these were primary sources of gold for Europe.</note> where **pure Gold** is brought as the most minute sand. But yours, being of a more noble grade, and yet in the form of a **mass** <note>Ingots or bars rather than naturally occurring dust.</note>, will not be without the greatest rumors. 

Buyers are not so stupid, even if, like playing children, they say: "Our eyes are closed; come, we do not see." Yet if you approach, they will see as much from even one corner of the eye as is enough to create the greatest misery for you. Indeed, **fine Silver** produced by our **Art** is brought from no known place. The best silver brought from **Spain** excels **English Sterling** <note>original: "Sterling Anglicanum"</note> by only a little in quality, and that comes in the form of rougher Coinage, which by theft...

# An Open Entrance
## To the King’s Closed Palace

<page-num>40</page-num>
<header>An Open Entrance</header>

...is transported, since the **Laws of the Lands** prohibit it. If, therefore, you sell an abundance of Pure Silver, you have already betrayed yourself; but if you debase it <note>Debasing or "adulterating" involved mixing precious metals with cheaper ones like copper, a common form of forgery.</note> (not being a licensed Metalworker), you are liable for the death penalty according to the **Laws of England** and **Holland** and nearly all nations. These laws provide that any deterioration of Gold and Silver—even if it meets the weight standard—is judged a capital crime unless performed by a professional and licensed Metalworker. 

We ourselves know this from experience. Once, when we attempted to sell as much as 600 pounds of the purest silver outside our own country, dressed as a Merchant—for we did not dare to debase it, as nearly every region has its own standard for the quality of Silver and Gold which Metalworkers know easily—it reached the point that if we claimed it was brought from here or there, they would immediately recognize the truth through an Assay <term>Assay: A chemical test (Proba) used to determine the purity and origin of a precious metal.</term> and seize the seller. Those to whom we offered it immediately said to us: "**Artificial Silver**" <note>original: "Artefactum Argentum"; implying it was made by alchemy rather than mined.</note>. When we asked why they asserted this, they replied nothing else than: "We have learned to distinguish Silver brought from **England, Spain, etc.**; but this is of no such kind." Hearing this, we secretly withdrew and left both the Silver and the payment behind, never to be reclaimed.

<column-break/>

<page-num>41</page-num>
<header>To the King's Closed Palace</header>

Moreover, if you pretend that a great weight of Gold, and especially Silver, was brought from elsewhere, this cannot happen without rumor. The Ship-captain will say, "Such a quantity of Silver was not brought by me," nor could it enter the ship without everyone's knowledge. And when others who are accustomed to trading there hear of it, they will laugh and say, "What? Is it likely that he could obtain such a mass of Silver or Gold here, and load it onto a ship, when the Laws are so strictly prohibitive and such a rigorous search is customary?" Thus, it will immediately be publicized, not just in one region, but in all those surrounding. 

Having been taught by these dangers, we have decided to remain hidden. To you, who dream of such an Art, we will communicate these things so that we may see what you will devise for the public good once you have become an **Adept** <term>Adept: A master who has successfully created the Philosopher's Stone.</term>. We say, therefore, that just as I previously taught that Mercury is necessary in the Work—and brought forth truths regarding Mercury which no antiquity before me has done—so now I notify you that Sulfur is required on the other hand. Without Sulfur, Mercury will never receive a profitable Coagulation <note>In alchemy, 'coagulation' refers to the process of turning a liquid or vaporous substance into a solid.</note> for the Supernatural Work. This Sulfur in our Work performs the role of the Male <note>original: "Maris vices gerit"; alchemy often used the metaphor of a marriage between the "male" Sulfur and "female" Mercury.</note>, and without this, whoever attempts the transmutatory Art tries all things in vain. All the Sages affirm that no Tincture <term>Tincture: The potent medicinal or transmutative liquid derived from the Stone.</term> can be made without its "Bronze" <note>original: "sine Ære suo"; a coded term used by ancient alchemists like Hermes to refer to the base matter of the work.</note>.

<summary>The author recounts a narrow escape from the law after trying to sell alchemical silver and explains why the secret must be kept. He then transitions into technical instruction, emphasizing that the "Male" principle of Sulfur is essential to solidify Mercury into the Stone.</summary>

which Bronze is called Gold without any ambiguity. Hence the noble **Sendivogius** <note>Michael Sendivogius (1566–1636), a famous Polish alchemist and physician</note> says: *“The one who knows,”* he says, *“recognizes our Stone even among dung, while the one who is ignorant does not believe it to be even in Gold.”* In Gold—that is, the <term>Gold of the Wise</term> <note>original: *Aurum Sophorum*; a refined or philosophical state of matter, not common metal</note>—the Tincture of Goldness lies hidden; although this is a most highly digested body, it is nevertheless <term>incrudated</term> <note>returned to a raw or "crude" state</note> in our <term>Mercury</term> alone, and from Mercury it receives the multiplication of its seed, not so much in weight as in virtue or power. And although many of the Wise seem to deny this as a deception, it is nevertheless true in reality, as I have said. 

They say, for instance, that common Gold is dead, while theirs is **Living**; just as a grain of wheat is "dead"—that is, the germinating activity in it lies suppressed and dormant—and so it would remain forever if it were kept in the surrounding dry air. But let it be cast into the earth, and it soon receives fermentative life: it swells, softens, and germinates. So it is with our Gold: it is "dead," meaning its life-giving force is sealed under a bodily husk, similar to a grain—though differently, insofar as there is a great difference between a vegetable grain and metallic gold. But just as the grain [remains] forever in dry air

unchanged, but is destroyed in fire, it is reducible in our **Water** alone, and then our grain is living; just as wheat sown in a field, its name changed, is the farmer’s ferment. While it remained in the granary, it was grain, suited both for bread-making and other uses as well as for sowing. Likewise Gold, as long as it is seen in the form of a ring, a vessel, or a coin, is common gold and is called "dead" in the former sense, because it would remain unchanged until the end of the world. In the latter sense, it is called "living" because it is thus in potential, which potential can be brought into action within a few days.

For just as a man is called "dead" who has already received a sentence of death, so Gold is called "living" when it is mixed in such a composition and placed under such a fire that it must necessarily receive germinative life shortly; indeed, within a few days, it will demonstrate the actions of beginning life. ### Open Entrance
<meta>catchword: so</meta>

if you are moved by the work and have prepared the **Agent**, and have rightly mixed your **Gold**, it will not be slow to become living: in which <term>Vivification</term> <note>original: *Vivificatione*; the process of bringing a "dead" metal back to a "living" or reactive state</note> your living <term>Menstruum</term> <note>original: *Menstruum*; a philosophical solvent, named after the monthly cycle to imply its role in "gestating" the stone</note> will die. From this it is clear that their Gold is taken as "dead," but the Water is "living," yet by composing these together through a brief <term>Digestion</term> <note>original: *Decoctione*; a slow boiling or heating process</note> the dead Gold is made alive, and the **Living Mercury** is killed—that is, the Spirit is thickened once the Body has been dissolved—and so both rot together in the form of a Sludge until all the members of the compound are torn apart into Atoms. This, then, is the Naturalness of our <term>Mastery</term> <note>original: *Magisterii*; the secret art of the Great Work</note>, the Mystery which we hide so diligently: to prepare the **Mercury**, truly so-called, which cannot be found upon the earth ready to our hands, and this for specific reasons known to the **Adepts**. In this Mercury we best amalgamate pure Gold—purified to the highest degree of purity, filed or made into thin plates—and, enclosed in Glass, we cook it steadily. The Gold is dissolved by the power of our Water and returns to its next [original] Matter, in which the Life of the Gold, though enclosed, may be free; and it receives the Life 

<column-break/>

# 45

### To the Closed Palace of the King
<meta>catchword: since</meta>

of the dissolving **Mercury**, which is, in respect to Gold, the same as good Earth is in respect to a grain of Wheat. In this Mercury, therefore, the dissolved Gold rots, and it must necessarily be so by the necessity of Nature. Therefore, after the rot of death, a **New Body** rises again, of the same Essence as the first but of a more noble Substance, which receives degrees of power in proportion to the difference between the qualities of the four **Elements**. This is the logic of our Work. This is our entire **Philosophy**. We say, therefore, that nothing is secret in our Work except for the Mercury alone; the Mastery of which consists in preparing it rightly, marrying it to the Gold in the just proportion, and governing the **Fire** according to the Mercury’s requirement. Because Gold by itself does not fear the fire, the Labor and the Work here is to accommodate the governance of the Heat to the tolerance of the Mercury. For whoever has not rightly prepared his Mercury, even if he has joined Gold with it, his Gold is still the Gold of the common people, inasmuch as it is joined with such a "foolish agent," in which it remains just as unchanged as if it were sitting in a chest, and by no governance of Fire will it set aside its bodily Nature. since that same Wheat remains in the granary as a food-supply, or the grain remains dead. For even if it is buried in a small box under the earth (just as the <note>original: *Indi Occidentales*; West Indians, referring to the indigenous peoples of the Americas</note> are accustomed to hide their grain in pits of the earth protected from water vapor), yet unless it meets with the moist Vapor of the earth, it is dead—that is, it remains without fruit, and is plainly removed from <term>Vegetation</term> <note>original: *Vegetatione*; in alchemy, this refers to the "growth" or multiplication of metals as if they were plants</note>. I know there are many who will criticize this doctrine, and will say: "The Gold of the common people is the material Subject of the Stone, and its running Mercury; but we know the contrary." Come now, you Philosophers, examine your purses! Because you "know" such things, do you possess the Stone? I indeed possess it; not by gift (unless from my GOD), nor by theft; I have it, I made it, and I keep it daily under my control. Go on, then, handle your rainwater, your May-dew, your Salts; prattle about your <term>Sperm</term> <note>original: *Spermate*; the "seed" or first matter of the stone</note> being more powerful than the Devil himself; provoke me with insults—do you believe I am affected by sadness by this foul talk of yours? I say that only **Gold** and **Mercury** are our Materials, and I know what I write, and GOD, the Searcher of Hearts, knows that I write the truth. Nor is there reason to accuse me of envy, since with an undaunted pen and in an unheard-of style, I write for the honor of GOD, the benefit of my Neighbor, and of the World

# 47

### To the Closed Palace of the King
<meta>catchword: our</meta>

and for the contempt of Riches. For <term>Elias the Artist</term> <note>original: *Elias Artista*; a legendary messianic figure in alchemy predicted by Paracelsus, who would reveal all secrets at the end of the world</note> is already born, and glorious things are already preached concerning the **City of GOD**. I would dare to assert that I possess more riches than the whole known World is worth; but it is not permitted to use them because of the snares of scoundrels. I rightly disdain and detest this "idol-mania" for Gold and Silver, with which the World celebrates price, pomp, and vanities. Ah, shameful crime! Ah, empty nothingness! Do you believe I hide these things out of envy? By no means; for I profess that I grieve from the bottom of my heart that we, as wanderers across the whole earth, are as if driven away from the Face of the Lord. But there is no need for words; what we have seen, touched, and worked on—what we have, possess, and know—these things we declare, moved only by compassion toward Students. It is out of indignation for Gold, Silver, and precious Stones—not insofar as they are God's Creatures (far from it, for in that respect we honor them), but because the People of Israel, as well as the worldlings, adore them. Wherefore, like the <note>original: *Serpentis instar ahenei*; a reference to the Brazen Serpent in the Bible (2 Kings 18:4) which was destroyed when people began to worship it as an idol</note> brazen serpent, let it be crushed into dust. I hope and expect that after a few years, money will be as common as cattle <note>original: *pecus erit pecunia*; a pun on the Latin words for cattle and money</note>, and this prop of the Antichristian beast will fall into ruins. The people are delirious, the nations are insane; they hold a useless weight in place of God. Does this our

## CHAPTER XIV.

### *Concerning the Accidental Circumstances Required in General for this Work.*

We have separated the <term>Chemical Art</term> <note>original: *Artem Chemicam*</note> from all common errors, and having conquered the sophistries and the curious dreams of those who merely think they know, we have taught that the Art must be made from **Gold** and **Mercury**. We have shown **Gold** alone without any metaphor; we have declared **Mercury** to be <term>Quicksilver</term> <note>original: *Argentum Vivum*</note>, beyond all ambiguity. We demonstrated the former to be perfected by nature and available for sale; we showed the latter is to be fashioned by Art. We have added reasons so clear and transparent that unless you wish to be blind in the midday sun, you cannot help but perceive them. We have professed, and we profess again: we have not brought these things forth based on the faith we give to the writings of others; we have seen and we have known the things we faithfully declare; we have made, seen, and we hold the great **Stone** of the **Elixir**. Nor indeed do we envy you the knowledge of it, but we wish that you might build your understanding from these writings. We have noted, moreover, that the preparation of the true <term>Philosophical Mercury</term> <note>original: *Mercurii veri Philosophici*</note> is difficult—so difficult that there is need of the special **Grace of God**, if anyone desires to reach the ex-

## CHAPTER XV.

### *Concerning the External Purgation of Mercury and Gold.*

Perfect **Gold** is dug out from the bowels of the Earth, where it is sometimes found in nuggets and sand. Then decant the Vinegar, and wash away the acidity with spring water, poured on repeatedly. Then dry the Mercury, and you will wonder at its brilliance. You could also wash it with Urine, or Vinegar and Salt, and forgo the Sublimation, then at least distill it four times after you have completed all the Eagles without further additions, washing the Steel Retort each time with Ash and Water; finally, boil it in distilled Vinegar for Half a Day, shaking it vigorously from time to time, and pour off the blackened Vinegar and pour on new

## CHAPTER XVII.
### *On the Proportion, Form, Material, and Closing of the Vessel.*

You shall have an oval and round glass <note>The "Philosopher's Egg," a specialized flask designed to withstand heat and circulate vapors.</note>, large enough to hold at least one ounce of distilled water in its sphere,

<column-break/>

<page-num>57</page-num>
<header>To the Closed Palace of the King.</header>
<sig>D 5</sig>
<meta>catchword: spon-</meta>

most, nor indeed less, if you can manage it; but you should obtain one as close to that measure as you carefully can. Let the glass have a neck of one palm in height <note>A "palm" is an informal measurement based on the width of the hand, roughly 3 to 4 inches.</note>; let the glass be very clear and thick—the thicker it is, the better—provided that you can distinguish the actions <note>The chemical changes and color shifts occurring inside the flask.</note> within the hollow of the glass. Let it not be thicker in one place than in another. Let the material adapted for this glass be half an ounce of Gold with one ounce of Mercury, and if you add triple the Mercury, the whole composition will still be within two ounces. And this proportion is exquisite. Furthermore, unless the glass is thick, it will not be able to endure in the fire, because the winds <note>The gases and high-pressure vapors generated during the heating process.</note> which are formed in the vessel from our embryo <note>The "embryo" is the starting mixture of gold and mercury that will "grow" into the Philosopher's Stone.</note> will burst the vessel. Let the glass be sealed at the top with such diligence and caution that there is no crack, let alone a hole, otherwise the work would perish. Thus you see that the work, in its material beginnings, does not exceed the price of three gold coins <note>original: "Aureorum Trium."</note>. Indeed, in the making of the "water" <note>The prepared mercury.</note>, the expense for one pound hardly exceeds two crowns. The instruments, I admit, are several, yet they are by no means expensive; and if you have my distillation instrument, you will easily be spared from fragile glasses. There are some, however, who dream that the price of perhaps a single Imperial coin <note>original: "Imperialis."</note> will serve the whole work, to whom I respond—

### ->To Chapter XVII.<-

He excludes here all Mercuries of the Moon, as well as those of the Sun; why this is done must be philosophically scrutinized. First, therefore, it must be known that in one Ounce of Gold or Silver there lies a 280th part (or thereabouts) which is of a Fixed <note>term: "Fixed" <gloss>A substance that remains stable and does not evaporate or change when exposed to intense heat.</gloss></note> Nature; the rest is Common Mercury. However, through Resuscitative Salts <note>Salts used in alchemical processing to "revive" or separate the internal essences of a metal.</note>, that coagulating essence—or that Fixed Essence—is attracted into the Salts, and the remainder is the Dead Mercury of the masses. It is otherwise in Our Mercury, whether of the Sun or Moon, where the Philosophical Mercury gently extracts and preserves that Essence, which otherwise nothing in the whole world can achieve.

## CHAPTER XVIII.
### On the Furnace or Sophic Athanor.

Concerning Mercury, enough has been said—of its Preparation, Proportion, and Virtue; likewise regarding Sulfur and its Necessity and Use in Our Work. I have warned how these are to be prepared and taught how they are to be mixed; likewise regarding the Vessel in which they are to be sealed, I have revealed much. I warn that all these things are to be understood with a grain of salt <note>original: "cum Grano Salis"; the author uses this common idiom to suggest that the literal instructions contain hidden allegorical meanings.</note>, lest perhaps by proceeding literally you more often [err...]

...lest one should happen to wander. For example, when we spoke without any Ambiguity of one of the Principles as **Mercury** and the other as the **Sun**; one as **Saleable**, the other as **to be Made by Art**: know that **Our Mercury** gives Gold of itself. If you do not know this—the Subject of our Secrets—you must sell it as **Common Gold**; and in every trial it is true Gold, and therefore it is saleable—that is, it can be sold to anyone without Scruple. Our Gold, therefore, is commonly saleable, but not commonly bought, because for it to be *Ours*, our **Art** is necessary. You might find **Our Sun** in common Gold and Silver <note>original: "Sole Lunaque Vulgari"; the alchemical terms for the metals gold and silver.</note>; I myself have sought and found it in these. But it is by no means an easy Task. The **Stone** itself is to be made with lighter Trouble than you will find the Stone's *immediate material* in gold bought commonly. Wherefore **Our Gold** is the *immediate material* of our Stone; the Gold of the Common people is the *near material*; other Metals are *remote*, and those things which are not Metallic are *most remote*, or rather *alien*. Because **Our Gold** is a <term>Chaos</term> <note>In alchemy, "Chaos" refers to the prime, unformed matter that contains all potentiality.</note>, whose **Soul** has not been put to flight by **Fire**. One must employ such a **Fire**, for instance, as we supply in the **Multiplication**, while the physical Gold or Silver of the Common people is employed as a <term>Ferment</term> <note>A substance added to a chemical mixture to trigger a transformation, similar to how yeast affects dough.</note> for completing the **Elixir**. This will certainly be a **Labyrinth**, unless you know how to extricate yourself. Nevertheless, in any Progress, you need an equal and continuous Heat, whether you have worked in **Common Gold** or in **Ours**. Know this about both: that **Our Gold** will give you the Work completed two or three Months sooner than the Gold of the Common people, and the **Elixir** in its first **Perfection** will be of a **Thousand-fold Virtue**, whereas in the other Work it will scarcely be of a **Hundred-fold**. you must illuminate and wax <note>original: "incerare"; to bring a substance to a waxy, fluid state during heating</note> that material, as is abundantly taught in the ***Great Rosary*** <note>Referring to the *Rosarium Philosophorum*, a famous 16th-century alchemical treatise</note>. Furthermore, if you have worked within **Our Sun**, you may calcine, putrefy, and whiten it, with the help of a benign internal **Fire**, along with a dewy **Warmth** administered from the outside. If you have worked with **Common Sun** <note>Common gold</note>, the **Materials** must be prepared by sublimating and boiling, so that you may later be able to unite them with the **Virgin’s Milk** <note>original: "Virginis Lacte"; a traditional term for a white mercurial solvent or secret water</note>. However you have made your Progress, you will be able to accomplish nothing whatsoever without **Fire**. Wherefore the truth-telling **Hermes** <note>Hermes Trismegistus, the legendary founder of alchemy</note> not without reason established **Fire** as the **Governor of the Work**, second only to the Sun and Moon. Yet I would have this fire understood as being truly from our secret **Furnace**, which the common **Eye** has never seen. There is, however, another **Furnace** which we call **Common**, which shall be constructed either of **Brick**, or of **Potter’s Clay**, or of **Iron or Bronze Plates** well-coated with **Clay**. We call this **Furnace** the **Athanor** <note>From the Arabic *al-tannur*; a self-feeding furnace designed to maintain a constant temperature for long periods</note>, whose **Form** most pleasing to me is the **Tower** with a **Nest**. Therefore, let there be a **Tower** of about three **Feet in Height**, and nine **Digits** wide, or a common **Span**; after the **Foundation** or base layer, let there be a **Little Door** for clearing out the **Ashes**, three or four **Digits** wide on all sides with a **Stone** fitted to it, immediately above which

<column-break/>

<page-num>63</page-num>
<header>To the Closed Palace of the King.</header>

the **Grate** is to be placed; slightly above the **Grate**, let there be two **Holes**, of about two **Digits**, through which the **Heat** may be sent into the attached **Athanor**. For the rest, let the **Tower** be exactly closed off from any **Cracks**; but from the top, the **Coals** are to be put in—those which are first lit, then others may be thrown in—then finally let the **Opening** be exactly sealed. In such a **Furnace**, you may complete the **Work** according to your **Heart’s Desire**. Moreover, if you are **Curious**, you may find various other **Ways** of administering the required **Fire**. Let the **Athanor** therefore be made in this **Manner**, so that after the **Matter** is placed inside, you may apply any **Degree of Heat** without the **Removal of the Glass**, according to your **Wish**, from a **Feverish Heat** <note>A low, gentle warmth like that of a body</note> even up to the **Fire** of a minor **Reverberatory** <note>An intense fire where heat is reflected back onto the matter</note>, and in its most intense **Degree**, it should last by itself for at least **Ten** or **Twelve Hours**. Then the **Gate of the Work** lies open to you. Truly, once you have gained possession of the **Stone**, you may more usefully fashion a **Portable Furnace**, because the **Stone**, once made, is multiplied in less **Time** and with a **Gentler Fire of Nature**.

### CHAPTER XIX.

## On the Progress of the Work through the First Forty Days.

Having prepared **Our Mercury** and **Our Sun** <note>The author distinguishes between "Common Gold" and the alchemical "Philosophical Gold" or "Our Sun"</note>, shut them in **Our Vessel**, and govern them with **Our Fire**, and within **Forty Days** you will see the whole **Matter** converted into a **Shadow**, that is, into **Atoms**, without any mover or visible motion, or any heat detectable by touch, except that it grows warm. Truly, if the mystery of **Our Sun** and **Our Mercury** remains hidden from you until now, take your hand from the work; for nothing but expense <note>Loss of money and effort</note> awaits you. But if you do not yet know the mystery of **Our Sun** in its full extent, yet have attained the knowledge of **Our Mercury**, then take one part of well-purified **Common Gold**, and three parts of **Our highly radiant Mercury**; join them, as was

<column-break/>

<page-num>65</page-num>
<header>To the Closed Palace of the King.</header>
<sig>E</sig>
<meta>catchword: de- (the start of the word "denique" or similar on the following page)</meta>

previously said, and place them on the fire, giving heat in which it may boil and sweat, and let its sweat be **Circulated** <note>Refers to the process of refluxing, where vapors rise, condense, and drip back down</note>, and this day and night for **Ninety Days and Nights**, and you will see that this **Mercury** has broken apart and again joined all the elements of **Common Gold**. Afterward, boil it for another **Fifty Days**, and you will see **Common Gold** converted by **Our Mercury** into **Our Sun**, which is the **Medicine of the First Order**. 

This **Sulphur** is now ours, but it does not yet tinge <note>It does not yet have the power to transform other metals into gold</note>. And believe me, many **Philosophers** have worked by this way, and have attained the truth; and it is a very tedious way, and it is for the **Magnates of the Earth** <note>The wealthy, who can afford the long, expensive process</note>, because having obtained this **Sulphur**, do not believe you have the **Stone**, but its true material, which you can seek and find in the **Imperfect Thing** within a week. This is **Our Way**, easy and rare, and God has reserved this for the **Poor and Despised**, and his cast-off **Saints**. 

Regarding this matter, I have now decided to speak many words, although at the beginning of this book I had decided to bury it in deep silence. This is one great **Sophism** <note>A riddle or deceptive paradox used by alchemists to hide their secrets</note> of all **Adepts**: some speak of the **Gold** and **Silver** of the **Vulgar** <note>Common gold and silver used in coinage</note>, and they speak the truth; and others deny the same, and they speak the truth. I, moved by **Charity**, now extend my hand, and I now call upon all **Adepts**, and I accuse them all of **Envy**. I also...

I had intended to tread that same **Path of Envy** <note>original: "Invidiæ semitam"; a common alchemical phrase referring to the "Envy of the Philosophers," where authors intentionally obscured their secrets to keep them from the unworthy</note>, except that **GOD** turned Us aside against Our own plan—to Whom be eternal Sanctification! I say, therefore, that both Ways are true, because there is only one Way in the **End**: though not in the **Beginning**. For the whole matter lies in **Our Mercury** and **Our Sun**. **Our Mercury** is our Way, and without it nothing will be achieved; **Our Sun** likewise is not the **Common Gold**, and yet it is found within it. And if you have worked with **Our Mercury** and **Common Gold** under the proper **Regimen** <term>Regimen: The systematic management of heat and time during the alchemical process</term>, within **One Hundred and Fifty Days** you will possess **Our Gold**, because **Our Sun** is derived from **Our Mercury**. Therefore, if **Common Gold** is broken down into its elements by **Our Mercury**, and joined together again, the entire mixture, by the benefit of **Fire**, will become **Our Gold**. If this **Gold** is then cooked again through **Mercury**, it will surely provide all the **Signs** described by the **Philosophers**, using such a **Fire** as they themselves have written about. 

But if you apply the **Regimen of the Stone** to the cooking of **Common Sun** <note>Gold</note>, however pure it may be, with **Our Mercury**, you are surely on the **Way of Error**. And here is that great **Labyrinth** in which almost all beginners get stuck, because the **Philosophers** write in their books about two Ways, which in reality are only **One Way**, except that one is

In all common metals, for example, is **Our Sun**; but it is contained more closely in **Gold** and **Silver**. Therefore, says **Flamel**, some have labored in **Jupiter** <note>Tin</note>, others in **Saturn** <note>Lead</note>, but I, *he says*, labored in the **Sun** <note>Gold</note> and found it.

Ripley <note>George Ripley (c. 1415–1490), a famous English alchemist and author of *The Compound of Alchymie*</note> teaches to rotate the Wheel <term>Rotating the Wheel: A metaphor for the repetition of alchemical cycles, such as distillation or circulation</term> a third time in the Chapter on **Calcination** <term>Calcination: The process of reducing a solid to a fine powder through heat, the first step of the Great Work</term>, where he explicitly speaks of the **Common Sun** <note>original: "Sole Vulgi," meaning common gold</note>, and he must be understood in this way. In the Doctrine of his Proportions, he is quite obscure, because those three Proportions serve three separate Works. 

One Work is most secret and purely natural; it is performed in **Our Mercury** with **Our Sun**, and to this Work must be attributed all the Signs described by the Sages. This Work is performed neither by fire nor by human Hands, but by internal Heat alone; external Heat only serves to expel the Cold and overcome its symptoms. 

The second Work is in purified Gold with **Our Mercury**, which is done with a glowing Fire over a long period of time, in which both are boiled down <term>Decoction: A prolonged heating or "cooking" of a substance to achieve maturity</term> through the mediation of **Venus** <note>Venus: The alchemical name for Copper</note>, until the purer substance of both is expressed, which is the **Juice of Lunaria** <term>Juice of Lunaria: A symbolic name for a purified mercurial extract, often associated with the silver or "white" stage of the work</term>. This juice must be taken after the dregs are cast aside, for it is not yet the Stone, but **Our True Sulfur**, which finally must be boiled down with **Our Mercury**—its own appropriated Blood—into the **Stone of Fire**, which is highly penetrating and staining <term>Tinging: The transformative power of the Stone to change the color and nature of base metals</term>.
